Normalize split placeholders by merging adjacent <hp:t> within same run (pragmatic global merge)\n        merged = re.sub(r'</hp:t>\\s*<hp:t>', '', raw)\n\n        # Replace placeholders\n        def repl(match: re.Match):\n            key = match.group(1)\n            if key not in values:\n                raise KeyError(f\"Missing JSON key for placeholder: {key} in {name}\")\n            return values[key]\n\n        replaced = re.sub(r'\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}', repl, merged)\n\n        #
